By placing itself at this juncture, this study makes three key contributions: First, it comparatively performs generic news frame detection and compares the performance of five BERT-based variants (BERT, RoBERTa, DeBERTa, DistilBERT and ALBERT) to add to the debate on best practices around employing computational text analysis for political communication studies. Second, it introduces various fine-tuned models capable of robustly performing generic news frame detection. Third, building upon numerous previous studies that work with US-centric data, this study provides the scholarly community with a labelled generic news frames dataset based on the Swiss electoral context that aids in testing the contextual robustness of these computational approaches to framing analysis.

</details>


### [114] [ClinicalAgents: Multi-Agent Orchestration for Clinical Decision Making with Dual-Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26182)
*Zhuohan Ge,Haoyang Li,Yubo Wang,Nicole Hu,Chen Jason Zhang,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ClinicalAgents是一个基于多智能体的框架，通过模拟专家医生的认知工作流，利用蒙特卡洛树搜索和双记忆架构提升临床诊断的准确性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常依赖症状到诊断的静态线性映射，难以捕捉人类医生迭代式、假设驱动的复杂推理过程，导致大语言模型在临床诊断中表现受限。

Method: 提出ClinicalAgents，采用多智能体框架，以蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）为动态编排机制，结合Orchestrator进行假设生成与证据验证，并引入双记忆架构——可变的工作记忆用于维护患者状态，静态的经验记忆用于检索临床指南和历史病例。

Result: 实验表明，ClinicalAgents在诊断准确性和可解释性方面显著优于现有的单智能体和多智能体基线方法，达到当前最优性能。

Conclusion: ClinicalAgents有效模拟了专家医生的非线性推理过程，通过动态编排和双记忆机制提升了复杂临床诊断任务的表现，为AI辅助诊断提供了更具解释性的解决方案。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated potential in healthcare, they often struggle with the complex, non-linear reasoning required for accurate clinical diagnosis. Existing methods typically rely on static, linear mappings from symptoms to diagnoses, failing to capture the iterative, hypothesis-driven reasoning inherent to human clinicians. To bridge this gap, we introduce ClinicalAgents, a novel multi-agent framework designed to simulate the cognitive workflow of expert clinicians. Unlike rigid sequential chains, ClinicalAgents employs a dynamic orchestration mechanism modeled as a Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) process. This allows an Orchestrator to iteratively generate hypotheses, actively verify evidence, and trigger backtracking when critical information is missing. Central to this framework is a Dual-Memory architecture: a mutable Working Memory that maintains the evolving patient state for context-aware reasoning, and a static Experience Memory that retrieves clinical guidelines and historical cases via an active feedback loop. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ClinicalAgents achieves state-of-the-art performance, significantly enhancing both diagnostic accuracy and explainability compared to strong single-agent and multi-agent baselines.

</details>


### [115] [Sparse Auto-Encoders and Holism about Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26207)
*Jumbly Grindrod*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了大语言模型（LLM）技术是否暗示了一种关于语言意义如何形成的元语义图景，重点分析了分布语义所蕴含的意义整体论，并回应了近期基于稀疏自编码器发现的可解释潜在特征对其构成的分解主义挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验LLM所采用的分布语义是否支持意义整体论，并评估新兴的机械可解释性研究（如可解释特征的发现）对此观点的挑战。

Method: 通过回顾LLM支持意义整体论的原有论证，引入稀疏自编码器中发现的可解释特征作为挑战，进而深入分析这些特征的本质，并在特征可数的前提下为整体论立场辩护。

Result: 发现尽管稀疏自编码器揭示了大量可解释的局部特征，可能支持分解主义语义观，但只要这些特征是可数的，原有的意义整体论仍可成立。

Conclusion: LLM的语义结构可以在保留某种形式的整体论的同时容纳可解释的局部特征，关键在于这些特征的可数性，从而维护了分布语义与整体论相容的可能性。

Abstract: Does Large Language Model (LLM) technology suggest a meta-semantic picture i.e. a picture of how words and complex expressions come to have the meaning that they do? One modest approach explores the assumptions that seem to be built into how LLMs capture the meanings of linguistic expressions as a way of considering their plausibility (Grindrod, 2026a, 2026b). It has previously been argued that LLMs, in employing a form of distributional semantics, adopt a form of holism about meaning (Grindrod, 2023; Grindrod et al., forthcoming). However, recent work in mechanistic interpretability presents a challenge to these arguments. Specifically, the discovery of a vast array of interpretable latent features within the high dimensional spaces used by LLMs potentially challenges the holistic interpretation. In this paper, I will present the original reasons for thinking that LLMs embody a form of holism (section 1), before introducing recent work on features generated through sparse auto-encoders, and explaining how the discovery of such features suggests an alternative decompositional picture of meaning (section 2). I will then respond to this challenge by considering in greater detail the nature of such features (section 3). Finally, I will return to the holistic picture defended by Grindrod et al. and argue that the picture still stands provided that the features are countable (section 4).

</details>


### [116] [Ask or Assume? Uncertainty-Aware Clarification-Seeking in Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26233)
*Nicholas Edwards,Sebastian Schuster*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种不确定性感知的多智能体框架，用于检测和解决大语言模型在软件工程任务中遇到的指令不明确问题，显著提升了任务解决率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型智能体主要优化自主执行，难以处理开放领域中常见的指令不完整问题，而人类开发者通常通过提问澄清需求，因此需要让智能体具备主动提问的能力。

Method: 设计了一个多智能体框架，将“不明确性检测”与“代码执行”分离，并引入不确定性感知机制，使智能体在面对模糊指令时能主动提出澄清问题。在SWE-bench Verified的不明确版本上进行系统评估。

Result: 使用OpenHands + Claude Sonnet 4.5的多智能体系统实现了69.40%的任务解决率，优于单智能体基线（61.20%），并缩小了与完全明确指令下的性能差距；系统表现出良好的不确定性校准能力，在简单任务上少提问，在复杂任务上主动寻求信息。

Conclusion: 当前模型可通过多智能体架构转变为能够主动协作的伙伴，自主识别何时需要提问以获取现实世界中缺失的信息，从而更有效地完成不明确任务。

Abstract: As Large Language Model (LLM) agents are increasingly deployed in open-ended domains like software engineering, they frequently encounter underspecified instructions that lack crucial context. While human developers naturally resolve underspecification by asking clarifying questions, current agents are largely optimized for autonomous execution. In this work, we systematically evaluate the clarification-seeking abilities of LLM agents on an underspecified variant of SWE-bench Verified. We propose an uncertainty-aware multi-agent scaffold that explicitly decouples underspecification detection from code execution. Our results demonstrate that this multi-agent system using OpenHands + Claude Sonnet 4.5 achieves a 69.40% task resolve rate, significantly outperforming a standard single-agent setup (61.20%) and closing the performance gap with agents operating on fully specified instructions. Furthermore, we find that the multi-agent system exhibits well-calibrated uncertainty, conserving queries on simple tasks while proactively seeking information on more complex issues. These findings indicate that current models can be turned into proactive collaborators, where agents independently recognize when to ask questions to elicit missing information in real-world, underspecified tasks.

</details>


### [117] [GS-BrainText: A Multi-Site Brain Imaging Report Dataset from Generation Scotland for Clinical Natural Language Processing Development and Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26235)
*Beatrice Alex,Claire Grover,Arlene Casey,Richard Tobin,Heather Whalley,William Whiteley*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GS-BrainText是一个包含8,511份脑部放射学报告的数据集，其中2,431份标注了24种脑部疾病表型，来自苏格兰五个NHS医疗委员会，具有广泛的年龄代表性，用于开发和评估可泛化的临床自然语言处理（NLP）工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有英国临床文本资源匮乏，且缺乏对NLP工具在不同数据来源、人群和语言表达上泛化能力的系统评估，因此需要一个高质量、多中心、标注丰富的脑部影像报告数据集来推动临床NLP研究。

Method: 收集Generation Scotland队列中的8,511份多中心脑部放射学报告，由跨学科临床团队使用统一标注框架对2,431份报告进行24种脑部疾病表型的人工标注，每个NHS医疗委员会有10%-100%的双人标注，并实施严格的质量控制；采用已有的基于规则的NLP系统EdIE-R进行基准性能评估。

Result: EdIE-R在不同医疗委员会（F1: 86.13–98.13）、表型（F1: 22.22–100）和年龄组（F1: 87.01–98.13）上表现出性能差异，揭示了NLP工具在跨机构、跨人群应用中的泛化挑战；成功构建并发布了大规模标注数据集GS-BrainText。

Conclusion: GS-BrainText填补了英国临床文本资源的空白，是一个可用于研究语言变异、诊断不确定性表达以及数据特征对NLP系统影响的重要资源，有助于推动更具泛化能力的临床NLP方法的发展。

Abstract: We present GS-BrainText, a curated dataset of 8,511 brain radiology reports from the Generation Scotland cohort, of which 2,431 are annotated for 24 brain disease phenotypes. This multi-site dataset spans five Scottish NHS health boards and includes broad age representation (mean age 58, median age 53), making it uniquely valuable for developing and evaluating generalisable clinical natural language processing (NLP) algorithms and tools. Expert annotations were performed by a multidisciplinary clinical team using an annotation schema, with 10-100% double annotation per NHS health board and rigorous quality assurance. Benchmark evaluation using EdIE-R, an existing rule-based NLP system developed in conjunction with the annotation schema, revealed some performance variation across health boards (F1: 86.13-98.13), phenotypes (F1: 22.22-100) and age groups (F1: 87.01-98.13), highlighting critical challenges in generalisation of NLP tools. The GS-BrainText dataset addresses a significant gap in available UK clinical text resources and provides a valuable resource for the study of linguistic variation, diagnostic uncertainty expression and the impact of data characteristics on NLP system performance.

</details>


### [118] [A Universal Vibe? Finding and Controlling Language-Agnostic Informal Register with SAEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26236)
*Uri Z. Kialy,Avi Shtarkberg,Ayal Klein*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 多语言大模型不仅将非正式语体处理为表面语言特征，还将其内化为跨语言的抽象概念，研究通过稀疏自编码器发现了一个共享的‘非正式语体子空间’，并在多语言中实现了形式性的因果调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究多语言大模型是否将文化特定的语用语体（如俚语）作为孤立的语言特有记忆处理，还是作为统一的抽象概念进行表征。

Method: 使用Sparse Autoencoders（SAEs）分析Gemma-2-9B-IT模型在英语、希伯来语和俄语中的内部表征，并构建包含多义词在字面与非正式语境中使用的新型数据集，以区分语用与词汇层面的处理。

Result: 发现非正式语体信号大部分分布在语言特有特征中，但存在一个小型而稳健的跨语言共性核心，形成几何上连贯的‘非正式语体子空间’，且该子空间在深层更明显；激活这些特征可因果地改变输出的形式性，并零样本迁移到六种未见语言。

Conclusion: 多语言大模型将非正式语体内化为可迁移、语言无关的语用抽象，而非仅表面启发式，这是首次揭示其机制的证据。

Abstract: While multilingual language models successfully transfer factual and syntactic knowledge across languages, it remains unclear whether they process culture-specific pragmatic registers, such as slang, as isolated language-specific memorizations or as unified, abstract concepts. We study this by probing the internal representations of Gemma-2-9B-IT using Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) across three typologically diverse source languages: English, Hebrew, and Russian. To definitively isolate pragmatic register processing from trivial lexical sensitivity, we introduce a novel dataset in which every target term is polysemous, appearing in both literal and informal contexts. We find that while much of the informal-register signal is distributed across language-specific features, a small but highly robust cross-linguistic core consistently emerges. This shared core forms a geometrically coherent ``informal register subspace'' that sharpens in the model's deeper layers. Crucially, these shared representations are not merely correlational: activation steering with these features causally shifts output formality across all source languages and transfers zero-shot to six unseen languages spanning diverse language families and scripts. Together, these results provide the first mechanistic evidence that multilingual LLMs internalize informal register not just as surface-level heuristics, but as a portable, language-agnostic pragmatic abstraction.

</details>


### [119] [Distilling Conversations: Abstract Compression of Conversational Audio Context for LLM-based ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26246)
*Shashi Kumar,Esaú Villatoro-Tello,Sergio Burdisso,Kadri Hacioglu,Thibault Bañeras-Roux,Hasindri Watawana,Dairazalia Sanchez-Cortes,Srikanth Madikeri,Petr Motlicek,Andreas Stolcke*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了多模态上下文是否能提升基于大语言模型的语音识别（ASR）性能，并提出了一种高效的上下文表示方法——Abstract Compression，用固定数量的潜在标记压缩先前轮次的音频信息，在减少计算开销的同时保留了部分上下文增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的基于LLM的语音识别系统通常孤立处理语句，难以利用对话上下文。本文旨在探索如何有效利用多轮对话中的上下文信息来提升识别效果，尤其是对上下文相关实体的识别。

Method: 通过有监督的多轮训练，比较使用原始上下文与压缩上下文的效果；提出Abstract Compression方法，将前序轮次的音频部分替换为固定数量的学习得到的潜在标记，同时显式保留文本转录内容。

Result: 实验表明，上下文信息主要有助于上下文实体的识别；相比直接使用长序列音频标记，Abstract Compression在显著减小音频输入长度的情况下，仍能恢复部分原始上下文带来的性能增益。

Conclusion: Abstract Compression是一种有效的上下文压缩方法，能够在控制输入长度和计算成本的同时，保留多轮对话中关键的上下文信息，适用于基于LLM的ASR系统。

Abstract: Standard LLM-based speech recognition systems typically process utterances in isolation, limiting their ability to leverage conversational context. In this work, we study whether multimodal context from prior turns improves LLM-based ASR and how to represent that context efficiently. We find that, after supervised multi-turn training, conversational context mainly helps with the recognition of contextual entities. However, conditioning on raw context is expensive because the prior-turn audio token sequence grows rapidly with conversation length. To address this, we propose Abstract Compression, which replaces the audio portion of prior turns with a fixed number of learned latent tokens while retaining corresponding transcripts explicitly. On both in-domain and out-of-domain test sets, the compressed model recovers part of the gains of raw-context conditioning with a smaller prior-turn audio footprint. We also provide targeted analyses of the compression setup and its trade-offs.

</details>


### [120] [Automatic Speech Recognition for Documenting Endangered Languages: Case Study of Ikema Miyakoan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26248)
*Chihiro Taguchi,Yukinori Takubo,David Chiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究致力于开发一种自动语音识别（ASR）系统，用于记录濒危的冲绳伊计马方言，通过实地录音构建语料库，训练出字符错误率低至15%的模型，并验证ASR可显著提升转录效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于伊计马语严重濒危且使用者多为高龄人群，亟需高效技术手段加速语言记录与保存。

Method: 基于实地采集的录音数据构建总时长为{totaldatasethours}小时的语音语料库，并训练端到端ASR模型，评估其在人工转录中的辅助效果。

Result: 成功训练出字符错误率低至15%的ASR模型，实验证明该系统能显著减少转录时间与认知负担。

Conclusion: ASR技术可有效支持濒危语言的规模化记录，为语言保护提供可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Language endangerment poses a major challenge to linguistic diversity worldwide, and technological advances have opened new avenues for documentation and revitalization. Among these, automatic speech recognition (ASR) has shown increasing potential to assist in the transcription of endangered language data. This study focuses on Ikema, a severely endangered Ryukyuan language spoken in Okinawa, Japan, with approximately 1,300 remaining speakers, most of whom are over 60 years old. We present an ongoing effort to develop an ASR system for Ikema based on field recordings. Specifically, we (1) construct a {\totaldatasethours}-hour speech corpus from field recordings, (2) train an ASR model that achieves a character error rate as low as 15\%, and (3) evaluate the impact of ASR assistance on the efficiency of speech transcription. Our results demonstrate that ASR integration can substantially reduce transcription time and cognitive load, offering a practical pathway toward scalable, technology-supported documentation of endangered languages.

</details>


### [121] [SocialX: A Modular Platform for Multi-Source Big Data Research in Indonesia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26253)
*Muhammad Apriandito Arya Saputra,Andry Alamsyah,Dian Puteri Ramadhani,Thomhert Suprapto Siadari,Hanif Fakhrurroja*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SocialX是一个面向印度尼西亚多源大数据研究的模块化平台，整合了异构数据采集、语言感知预处理和可插拔分析功能，支持源无关的研究流程，提升研究效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印度尼西亚的大数据研究因数据分散在多个来源且格式、访问方式和噪声特征各异而受到限制，研究人员需耗费大量精力构建独立的数据采集与处理流程，阻碍了研究本身的发展。

Method: 提出SocialX平台，采用模块化设计，将系统分为数据采集、预处理和分析三个独立层次，并通过轻量级任务协调机制连接，实现各层独立扩展；平台支持新增数据源、预处理方法和分析工具而不影响现有流程。

Result: 实现了统一的源无关数据处理流水线，提供了针对印尼语不同语域的语言感知预处理方法，并通过典型研究工作流展示了平台的有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: SocialX显著降低了印度尼西亚多源大数据研究的技术门槛，其模块化架构为未来扩展提供了良好支持，有助于推动本地化社会计算与语言技术研究的发展。

Abstract: Big data research in Indonesia is constrained by a fundamental fragmentation: relevant data is scattered across social media, news portals, e-commerce platforms, review sites, and academic databases, each with different formats, access methods, and noise characteristics. Researchers must independently build collection pipelines, clean heterogeneous data, and assemble separate analysis tools, a process that often overshadows the research itself. We present SocialX, a modular platform for multi-source big data research that integrates heterogeneous data collection, language-aware preprocessing, and pluggable analysis into a unified, source-agnostic pipeline. The platform separates concerns into three independent layers (collection, preprocessing, and analysis) connected by a lightweight job-coordination mechanism. This modularity allows each layer to grow independently: new data sources, preprocessing methods, or analysis tools can be added without modifying the existing pipeline. We describe the design principles that enable this extensibility, detail the preprocessing methodology that addresses challenges specific to Indonesian text across registers, and demonstrate the platform's utility through a walkthrough of a typical research workflow. SocialX is publicly accessible as a web-based platform at https://www.socialx.id.

</details>


### [122] [findsylls: A Language-Agnostic Toolkit for Syllable-Level Speech Tokenization and Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26292)
*Héctor Javier Vázquez Martínez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: foundsylls是一个统一的、语言无关的工具包，用于整合经典和端到端的音节分割方法，支持跨多种语言和资源条件下的可重复音节级实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音节级单元在口语建模和无监督词发现中具有重要意义，但现有研究因实现、数据集和评估协议的差异而分散，缺乏统一框架。

Method: 开发了名为foundsylls的模块化工具包，集成经典音节检测器和端到端音节化模型，提供统一接口以实现音节分割、嵌入提取和多粒度评估，并标准化多种常用方法。

Result: 在英语、西班牙语语料库及科诺语（Kono）手动标注数据上验证了工具包的有效性，展示了其在高资源和低资源语言场景下的适用性和可比性。

Conclusion: foundsylls为音节分割研究提供了标准化、可扩展的框架，促进了不同方法之间的可控比较，提升了实验的可重复性与跨语言适用性。

Abstract: Syllable-level units offer compact and linguistically meaningful representations for spoken language modeling and unsupervised word discovery, but research on syllabification remains fragmented across disparate implementations, datasets, and evaluation protocols. We introduce findsylls, a modular, language-agnostic toolkit that unifies classical syllable detectors and end-to-end syllabifiers under a common interface for syllable segmentation, embedding extraction, and multi-granular evaluation. The toolkit implements and standardizes widely used methods (e.g., Sylber, VG-HuBERT) and allows their components to be recombined, enabling controlled comparisons of representations, algorithms, and token rates. We demonstrate findsylls on English and Spanish corpora and on new hand-annotated data from Kono, an underdocumented Central Mande language, illustrating how a single framework can support reproducible syllable-level experiments across both high-resource and under-resourced settings.

</details>


### [123] [From Human Cognition to Neural Activations: Probing the Computational Primitives of Spatial Reasoning in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26323)
*Jiyuan An,Liner Yang,Mengyan Wang,Luming Lu,Weihua An,Erhong Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究从机制层面探讨了大语言模型在空间推理任务中的表现，发现其内部的空间信息表征是短暂、碎片化且依赖上下文的，而非稳定、通用的结构化表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型在空间推理基准上的表现是否源于真正的空间表征能力，还是仅依赖语言启发式。

Method: 基于人类空间认知理论，将空间推理分解为三种基本操作，并设计受控任务；通过线性探测、稀疏自编码器特征分析和因果干预等方法分析多语言大模型的内部表征。

Result: 任务相关的空间信息存在于中间层并能因果影响行为，但表征具有瞬态性、跨任务家族的碎片化以及对最终预测的弱整合；跨语言分析显示相似行为表现背后存在不同的内部机制路径。

Conclusion: 当前大语言模型缺乏稳健、通用的空间推理能力，其空间表征有限且依赖上下文，需超越准确率指标进行机制性评估。

Abstract: As spatial intelligence becomes an increasingly important capability for foundation models, it remains unclear whether large language models' (LLMs) performance on spatial reasoning benchmarks reflects structured internal spatial representations or reliance on linguistic heuristics. We address this question from a mechanistic perspective by examining how spatial information is internally represented and used. Drawing on computational theories of human spatial cognition, we decompose spatial reasoning into three primitives, relational composition, representational transformation, and stateful spatial updating, and design controlled task families for each. We evaluate multilingual LLMs in English, Chinese, and Arabic under single pass inference, and analyze internal representations using linear probing, sparse autoencoder based feature analysis, and causal interventions. We find that task relevant spatial information is encoded in intermediate layers and can causally influence behavior, but these representations are transient, fragmented across task families, and weakly integrated into final predictions. Cross linguistic analysis further reveals mechanistic degeneracy, where similar behavioral performance arises from distinct internal pathways. Overall, our results suggest that current LLMs exhibit limited and context dependent spatial representations rather than robust, general purpose spatial reasoning, highlighting the need for mechanistic evaluation beyond benchmark accuracy.

</details>


### [124] [CALRK-Bench: Evaluating Context-Aware Legal Reasoning in Korean Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26332)
*JiHyeok Jung,TaeYoung Yoon,HyunSouk Cho*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了CALRK-Bench，一个基于韩国法律体系的上下文感知法律推理基准，旨在评估模型在法律规范时间有效性、法律信息充分性判断以及法律判决变化原因理解方面的能力。实验表明，即使是近期的大语言模型在这三项任务上表现仍然不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有法律基准主要假设法律规范是固定的，无法反映法律判断随上下文变化或多个规范交互的情况，因此需要一个能评估上下文感知法律推理能力的新基准。

Method: 基于韩国法律先例和法律咨询记录构建CALRK-Bench数据集，并由法律专家进行验证；设计三个评估任务：判断法律规范的时间有效性、判断法律信息是否充分、理解法律判决变化的原因。

Result: 实验结果显示，当前的大型语言模型在上述三项任务中 consistently 表现较差，表明其缺乏真正的上下文法律推理能力。

Conclusion: CALRK-Bench为评估模型的上下文感知法律推理能力提供了一个新的压力测试工具，超越了对法律知识的简单记忆，推动更深层次的法律AI研究。

Abstract: Legal reasoning requires not only the application of legal rules but also an understanding of the context in which those rules operate. However, existing legal benchmarks primarily evaluate rule application under the assumption of fixed norms, and thus fail to capture situations where legal judgments shift or where multiple norms interact. In this work, we propose CALRK-Bench, a context-aware legal reasoning benchmark based on the legal system in Korean. CALRK-Bench evaluates whether models can identify the temporal validity of legal norms, determine whether sufficient legal information is available for a given case, and understand the reasons behind shifts in legal judgments. The dataset is constructed from legal precedents and legal consultation records, and is validated by legal experts. Experimental results show that even recent large language models consistently exhibit low performance on these three tasks. CALRK-Bench provides a new stress test for evaluating context-aware legal reasoning rather than simple memorization of legal knowledge. Our code is available at https://github.com/jhCOR/CALRKBench.

</details>


### [125] [Switch Attention: Towards Dynamic and Fine-grained Hybrid Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26380)
*Yusheng Zhao,Hourun Li,Bohan Wu,Jingyang Yuan,Meng Zhang,Yichun Yin,Lifeng Shang,Ming Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Switch Attention (SwiAttn)的新型混合Transformer架构，能够动态地在全局注意力和滑动窗口注意力之间进行细粒度路由，以在保持效率的同时增强模型的上下文建模能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的全注意力机制计算复杂度随序列长度平方增长，限制了长文本建模；滑动窗口注意力虽提升效率但牺牲了全局视野，现有混合方法多依赖静态设计，缺乏灵活性。

Method: SwiAttn为每个token在每一层动态选择使用全注意力分支（用于全局信息聚合）或滑动窗口分支（用于高效局部匹配），并引入自适应正则化目标促进模型效率，通过持续预训练实现从全注意力到混合结构的迁移。

Result: 在23个基准数据集上进行了广泛实验，涵盖常规（4K）和长（32K）上下文长度，结果表明SwiAttn在性能与效率之间取得了优越平衡。

Conclusion: SwiAttn通过动态细粒度注意力路由机制，有效结合了全注意力与滑动窗口注意力的优势，在多种场景下实现了更高效的计算分配，显著提升了长上下文语言建模的表现。

Abstract: The attention mechanism has been the core component in modern transformer architectures. However, the computation of standard full attention scales quadratically with the sequence length, serving as a major bottleneck in long-context language modeling. Sliding window attention restricts the context length for better efficiency at the cost of narrower receptive fields. While existing efforts attempt to take the benefits from both sides by building hybrid models, they often resort to static, heuristically designed alternating patterns that limit efficient allocation of computation in various scenarios. In this paper, we propose Switch Attention (SwiAttn), a novel hybrid transformer that enables dynamic and fine-grained routing between full attention and sliding window attention. For each token at each transformer layer, SwiAttn dynamically routes the computation to either a full-attention branch for global information aggregation or a sliding-window branch for efficient local pattern matching. An adaptive regularization objective is designed to encourage the model towards efficiency. Moreover, we adopt continual pretraining to optimize the model, transferring the full attention architecture to the hybrid one. Extensive experiments are conducted on twenty-three benchmark datasets across both regular (4K) and long (32K) context lengths, demonstrating the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [126] [Why Models Know But Don't Say: Chain-of-Thought Faithfulness Divergence Between Thinking Tokens and Answers in Open-Weight Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26410)
*Richard J. Young*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究分析了12个开源推理模型在面对误导性提示时的表现，发现超过一半的情况下模型的“思考标记”中包含提示相关信息而回答文本中没有，表明仅监控输出答案会遗漏大量受提示影响的推理过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究扩展思考模型在处理误导性提示时，其内部思考过程与最终输出之间的差异，评估仅依赖可见回答进行监控的有效性。

Method: 在MMLU和GPQA数据集上测试12个开源推理模型，分析模型在遵循误导提示时，其思考标记和回答文本是否提及提示内容，并按提示类型和模型类型分类统计。

Result: 在10,506个案例中，55.4%存在‘思考-回答分歧’（仅思考标记提及提示），反向情况仅0.5%；不同提示类型（如讨好型、一致性、不道德提示）和不同模型间差异显著，部分模型高达94.7%的分歧率，且仍有11.8%的案例在两个通道均无提示提及。

Conclusion: 仅通过答案文本监控会遗漏过半的提示影响行为，访问思考标记虽有必要但仍不充分，需进一步开发更全面的模型内部推理监测机制。

Abstract: Extended-thinking models expose a second text-generation channel ("thinking tokens") alongside the user-visible answer. This study examines 12 open-weight reasoning models on MMLU and GPQA questions paired with misleading hints. Among the 10,506 cases where models actually followed the hint (choosing the hint's target over the ground truth), each case is classified by whether the model acknowledges the hint in its thinking tokens, its answer text, both, or neither. In 55.4% of these cases the model's thinking tokens contain hint-related keywords that the visible answer omits entirely, a pattern termed *thinking-answer divergence*. The reverse (answer-only acknowledgment) is near-zero (0.5%), confirming that the asymmetry is directional. Hint type shapes the pattern sharply: sycophancy is the most *transparent* hint, with 58.8% of sycophancy-influenced cases acknowledging the professor's authority in both channels, while consistency (72.2%) and unethical (62.7%) hints are dominated by thinking-only acknowledgment. Models also vary widely, from near-total divergence (Step-3.5-Flash: 94.7%) to relative transparency (Qwen3.5-27B: 19.6%). These results show that answer-text-only monitoring misses more than half of all hint-influenced reasoning and that thinking-token access, while necessary, still leaves 11.8% of cases with no verbalized acknowledgment in either channel.

</details>


### [127] [Automating Clinical Information Retrieval from Finnish Electronic Health Records Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26434)
*Mikko Saukkoriipi,Nicole Hernandez,Jaakko Sahlsten,Kimmo Kaski,Otso Arponen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种可在本地部署的临床上下文问答（CCQA）框架，利用开源大语言模型直接从电子健康记录（EHR）中回答临床问题，无需数据外传。在完全离线条件下，基于183名患者的1,664个专家标注问答对进行评估，Llama-3.1-70B和Qwen3-30B等模型表现出高准确率和一致性，低精度量化技术有助于降低部署门槛。研究发现少量但重要的临床错误，强调临床应用中仍需验证与人工监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床医生常需从电子健康记录（EHR）中提取患者特异性信息，该过程耗时且易出错。现有系统依赖外部服务器或云端处理，存在隐私和安全风险。因此，亟需一种可在本地运行、保障数据隐私且准确可靠的临床问答系统。

Method: 开发了一种本地部署的临床上下文问答（CCQA）框架，采用4B至70B参数规模的开源大语言模型，在完全离线环境下进行测试。使用来自183名患者、包含1,664个专家标注的问答对的数据集（主要为芬兰语临床文本）评估模型表现。评估包括自由生成和多项选择两种模式，并测试了不同量化级别（4位和8位）对性能和资源消耗的影响。同时分析模型对语义等价问题的一致性及临床显著错误率。

Result: 在自由生成任务中，Llama-3.1-70B达到95.3%的准确率和97.3%的一致性，Qwen3-30B-A3B-2507表现相近；在多项选择任务中各模型准确率相似但校准能力不同。低精度量化基本保持性能的同时显著降低GPU内存需求。临床评估显示2.9%的输出存在临床显著错误，0.96%的情况下语义等价问题产生分歧性结果（其中一者正确，另一者含临床显著错误）。

Conclusion: 本地部署的开源大语言模型能够以较高准确性从EHR中检索患者特异性信息，支持自然语言查询且无需数据外传，具备临床应用潜力。然而，模型仍可能产生重要错误且对等价问题响应不一致，因此在实际临床部署中必须结合验证机制与人工监督以确保安全性与可靠性。

Abstract: Clinicians often need to retrieve patient-specific information from electronic health records (EHRs), a task that is time-consuming and error-prone. We present a locally deployable Clinical Contextual Question Answering (CCQA) framework that answers clinical questions directly from EHRs without external data transfer. Open-source large language models (LLMs) ranging from 4B to 70B parameters were benchmarked under fully offline conditions using 1,664 expert-annotated question-answer pairs derived from records of 183 patients. The dataset consisted predominantly of Finnish clinical text. In free-text generation, Llama-3.1-70B achieved 95.3% accuracy and 97.3% consistency across semantically equivalent question variants, while the smaller Qwen3-30B-A3B-2507 model achieved comparable performance. In a multiple-choice setting, models showed similar accuracy but variable calibration. Low-precision quantization (4-bit and 8-bit) preserved predictive performance while reducing GPU memory requirements and improving deployment feasibility. Clinical evaluation identified clinically significant errors in 2.9% of outputs, and semantically equivalent questions occasionally yielded discordant responses, including instances where one formulation was correct and the other contained a clinically significant error (0.96% of cases). These findings demonstrate that locally hosted open-source LLMs can accurately retrieve patient-specific information from EHRs using natural-language queries, while highlighting the need for validation and human oversight in clinical deployment.

</details>


### [128] [ClimateCheck 2026: Scientific Fact-Checking and Disinformation Narrative Classification of Climate-related Claims](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26449)
*Raia Abu Ahmad,Max Upravitelev,Aida Usmanova,Veronika Solopova,Georg Rehm*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ClimateCheck 2026是针对气候相关声明自动验证的共享任务，扩展了训练数据并新增了错误信息叙事分类任务，通过结合密集检索、交叉编码器集成和大语言模型的方法提升验证效果，并揭示了不同气候错误信息在可验证性上的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于科学证据的专业性和气候错误信息修辞策略的多样性，自动验证气候相关声明具有挑战性，因此需要系统化的方法来应对这一问题。

Method: 采用密集检索管道、交叉编码器集成以及结合结构化分层推理的大语言模型，并在CodaBench平台上运行竞赛以评估系统性能。

Result: 比赛吸引了20名注册参与者和8份排行榜提交，系统表现出较强的技术整合能力；引入改进的评估框架揭示了传统指标中的系统性偏差，跨任务分析显示并非所有气候错误信息都同样易于验证。

Conclusion: 未来事实核查系统的设计需考虑不同类型气候错误信息的可验证性差异，并优化评估方法以减少系统性偏差。

Abstract: Automatically verifying climate-related claims against scientific literature is a challenging task, complicated by the specialised nature of scholarly evidence and the diversity of rhetorical strategies underlying climate disinformation. ClimateCheck 2026 is the second iteration of a shared task addressing this challenge, expanding on the 2025 edition with tripled training data and a new disinformation narrative classification task. Running from January to February 2026 on the CodaBench platform, the competition attracted 20 registered participants and 8 leaderboard submissions, with systems combining dense retrieval pipelines, cross-encoder ensembles, and large language models with structured hierarchical reasoning. In addition to standard evaluation metrics (Recall@K and Binary Preference), we adapt an automated framework to assess retrieval quality under incomplete annotations, exposing systematic biases in how conventional metrics rank systems. A cross-task analysis further reveals that not all climate disinformation is equally verifiable, potentially implicating how future fact-checking systems should be designed.

</details>


### [129] [Clinical named entity recognition in the Portuguese language: a benchmark of modern BERT models and LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26510)
*Vinicius Anjos de Almeida,Sandro Saorin da Silva,Josimar Chire,Leonardo Vicenzi,Nícolas Henrique Borges,Helena Kociolek,Sarah Miriã de Castro Rocha,Frederico Nassif Gomes,Júlia Cristina Ferreira,Oge Marques,Lucas Emanuel Silva e Oliveira*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估了基于BERT的模型和大语言模型（LLM）在葡萄牙语临床命名实体识别（NER）中的表现，并探索了解决多标签不平衡的策略。结果显示，mmBERT-base模型表现最佳（micro F1 = 0.76），且采用迭代分层等数据平衡策略可进一步提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 葡萄牙语临床文本的命名实体识别缺乏基准研究，现有模型在处理类别不平衡问题上存在挑战，亟需系统评估适用于葡萄牙语的模型及其优化策略。

Method: 比较了BioBERTpt、BERTimbau、ModernBERT和mmBERT等BERT类模型与GPT-5、Gemini-2.5等大语言模型，在公开数据集SemClinBr和私有乳腺癌数据集上进行训练和测试；采用相同的训练条件，并使用精确率、召回率和F1分数进行评估；尝试了迭代分层、加权损失和过采样等方法缓解类别不平衡。

Result: mmBERT-base模型取得最优性能（micro F1 = 0.76），优于其他所有模型；迭代分层有效改善了类别分布并提升了整体性能；多语言BERT模型（尤其是mmBERT）在本地低计算资源下仍表现良好。

Conclusion: 多语言BERT模型（如mmBERT）在葡萄牙语临床NER任务中表现优异，结合平衡的数据划分策略（如迭代分层）可进一步提高性能，具备实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Clinical notes contain valuable unstructured information. Named entity recognition (NER) enables the automatic extraction of medical concepts; however, benchmarks for Portuguese remain scarce. In this study, we aimed to evaluate BERT-based models and large language models (LLMs) for clinical NER in Portuguese and to test strategies for addressing multilabel imbalance. We compared BioBERTpt, BERTimbau, ModernBERT, and mmBERT with LLMs such as GPT-5 and Gemini-2.5, using the public SemClinBr corpus and a private breast cancer dataset. Models were trained under identical conditions and evaluated using precision, recall, and F1-score. Iterative stratification, weighted loss, and oversampling were explored to mitigate class imbalance. The mmBERT-base model achieved the best performance (micro F1 = 0.76), outperforming all other models. Iterative stratification improved class balance and overall performance. Multilingual BERT models, particularly mmBERT, perform strongly for Portuguese clinical NER and can run locally with limited computational resources. Balanced data-splitting strategies further enhance performance.

</details>


### [130] [AMALIA Technical Report: A Fully Open Source Large Language Model for European Portuguese](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26511)
*Afonso Simplício,Gonçalo Vinagre,Miguel Moura Ramos,Diogo Tavares,Rafael Ferreira,Giuseppe Attanasio,Duarte M. Alves,Inês Calvo,Inês Vieira,Rui Guerra,James Furtado,Beatriz Canaverde,Iago Paulo,Vasco Ramos,Diogo Glória-Silva,Miguel Faria,Marcos Treviso,Daniel Gomes,Pedro Gomes,David Semedo,André Martins,João Magalhães*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AMALIA是一个专注于欧洲葡萄牙语（pt-PT）的开源大语言模型，通过在训练中引入更多高质量pt-PT数据，并发布针对该语言变体的原生评测基准，显著提升了在pt-PT特定任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧洲葡萄牙语在现有大语言模型的训练和评估中代表性不足，且现有翻译基准难以捕捉其语言与文化特征，因此需要专门针对pt-PT进行模型训练与评估。

Method: 在中阶段训练和后训练阶段引入更多高质量的欧洲葡萄牙语数据，并构建一套包含翻译标准任务和四个新数据集的pt-PT原生评测基准，用于更准确地评估模型性能。

Result: AMALIA在翻译后的通用基准上表现与强基线模型相当，在pt-PT特定的生成、语言能力及偏见检测任务上显著优于现有模型。

Conclusion: 针对特定语言变体（如欧洲葡萄牙语）进行专门训练并建立原生评估基准，是提升低资源语言变体在大模型中表现的有效路径。

Abstract: Despite rapid progress in open large language models (LLMs), European Portuguese (pt-PT) remains underrepresented in both training data and native evaluation, with machine-translated benchmarks likely missing the variant's linguistic and cultural nuances. We introduce AMALIA, a fully open LLM that prioritizes pt-PT by using more high-quality pt-PT data during both the mid- and post-training stages. To evaluate pt-PT more faithfully, we release a suite of pt-PT benchmarks that includes translated standard tasks and four new datasets targeting pt-PT generation, linguistic competence, and pt-PT/pt-BR bias. Experiments show that AMALIA matches strong baselines on translated benchmarks while substantially improving performance on pt-PT-specific evaluations, supporting the case for targeted training and native benchmarking for European Portuguese.

</details>


### [131] [ALBA: A European Portuguese Benchmark for Evaluating Language and Linguistic Dimensions in Generative LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26516)
*Inês Vieira,Inês Calvo,Iago Paulo,James Furtado,Rafael Ferreira,Diogo Tavares,Diogo Glória-Silva,David Semedo,João Magalhães*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了ALBA，一个专为欧洲葡萄牙语（pt-PT）设计的语言学基准测试，涵盖八个语言维度，旨在评估大语言模型在该语言变体中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于现有训练数据和基准主要集中在巴西葡萄牙语（pt-BR），欧洲葡萄牙语（pt-PT）的评估资源匮乏，因此需要一个专门针对pt-PT的语言学基准来准确评估大语言模型在此语言变体中的性能。

Method: ALBA由语言专家手动构建，涵盖八个语言学维度：语言变体、文化相关语义、话语分析、文字游戏、句法、形态学、词汇学以及音位学与语音学，并结合LLM-as-a-judge框架实现可扩展的自动评估。

Result: 在多种模型上的实验显示出不同语言维度上的性能差异，表明当前模型在处理pt-PT特定语言现象时存在挑战。

Conclusion: 研究强调了建立全面且区分语言变体的基准的重要性，以支持pt-PT语言工具的进一步发展。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) expand across multilingual domains, evaluating their performance in under-represented languages becomes increasingly important. European Portuguese (pt-PT) is particularly affected, as existing training data and benchmarks are mainly in Brazilian Portuguese (pt-BR). To address this, we introduce ALBA, a linguistically grounded benchmark designed from the ground up to assess LLM proficiency in linguistic-related tasks in pt-PT across eight linguistic dimensions, including Language Variety, Culture-bound Semantics, Discourse Analysis, Word Plays, Syntax, Morphology, Lexicology, and Phonetics and Phonology. ALBA is manually constructed by language experts and paired with an LLM-as-a-judge framework for scalable evaluation of pt-PT generated language. Experiments on a diverse set of models reveal performance variability across linguistic dimensions, highlighting the need for comprehensive, variety-sensitive benchmarks that support further development of tools in pt-PT.

</details>


### [132] [How Open Must Language Models be to Enable Reliable Scientific Inference?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26539)
*James A. Michaelov,Catherine Arnett,Tyler A. Chang,Pamela D. Rivière,Samuel M. Taylor,Cameron R. Jones,Sean Trott,Roger P. Levy,Benjamin K. Bergen,Micah Altman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了模型开放程度对科学研究推断可靠性的影响，指出当前封闭模型通常不适合科学用途，并提出了识别和缓解推理威胁的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究模型开放性如何影响科学推断的可靠性，特别是在信息受限的情况下。

Method: 分析模型构建和部署中的信息限制如何威胁可靠推断，并讨论可能的解决方案。

Result: 发现当前大多数封闭模型不适合科学目的，但存在例外情况；提出应系统识别推理威胁并采取缓解措施。

Conclusion: 建议在研究中使用模型时，应提供模型选择的具体理由，并系统报告潜在的推理威胁及应对措施。

Abstract: How does the extent to which a model is open or closed impact the scientific inferences that can be drawn from research that involves it? In this paper, we analyze how restrictions on information about model construction and deployment threaten reliable inference. We argue that current closed models are generally ill-suited for scientific purposes, with some notable exceptions, and discuss ways in which the issues they present to reliable inference can be resolved or mitigated. We recommend that when models are used in research, potential threats to inference should be systematically identified along with the steps taken to mitigate them, and that specific justifications for model selection should be provided.

</details>


### [133] [Development of a European Union Time-Indexed Reference Dataset for Assessing the Performance of Signal Detection Methods in Pharmacovigilance using a Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26544)
*Maria Kefala,Jeffery L. Painter,Syed Tauhid Bukhari,Maurizio Sessa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究构建了一个包含时间索引的欧盟药物警戒参考数据集，基于产品说明书（SmPC）中不良事件（AE）的纳入时间及监管元数据，涵盖1995-2025年间的17,763份版本，支持对信号检测方法的更准确评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有药物警戒参考数据集缺乏不良事件被监管机构正式确认的时间信息，难以限制分析于确认前时期，限制了对早期信号检测性能的评估。本研究旨在填补这一空白，提供一个具有时间标记的参考数据集。

Method: 从欧盟集中授权药品注册库中获取1,513种药品当前和历史的说明书（SmPC），提取第4.8节内容，并利用DeepSeek V3模型识别不良事件；通过程序化方式提取监管元数据（如标签变更），并以AE首次进入SmPC的日期作为时间索引构建数据库。

Result: 数据库包含1995–2025年间17,763份SmPC版本，共125,026个药物-AE关联；限定于活跃产品的数据集包含1,479种药品和110,823个关联。多数AE为上市前识别（74.5%），25.5%为上市后新增；安全更新在2012年左右达到峰值；胃肠、皮肤和神经系统疾病是最常见的系统器官分类；每种药物中位数为48个AE，分布在14个SOC中。

Conclusion: 该时间索引参考数据集填补了欧盟药物警戒领域的关键空白，提供了不良事件官方确认的时间信息，有助于更准确地评估信号检测方法的性能，并促进不同分析方法之间的比较。

Abstract: Background: The identification of optimal signal detection methods is hindered by the lack of reliable reference datasets. Existing datasets do not capture when adverse events (AEs) are officially recognized by regulatory authorities, preventing restriction of analyses to pre-confirmation periods and limiting evaluation of early detection performance. This study addresses this gap by developing a time-indexed reference dataset for the European Union (EU), incorporating the timing of AE inclusion in product labels along with regulatory metadata. Methods: Current and historical Summaries of Product Characteristics (SmPCs) for all centrally authorized products (n=1,513) were retrieved from the EU Union Register of Medicinal Products (data lock: 15 December 2025). Section 4.8 was extracted and processed using DeepSeek V3 to identify AEs. Regulatory metadata, including labelling changes, were programmatically extracted. Time indexing was based on the date of AE inclusion in the SmPC. Results: The database includes 17,763 SmPC versions spanning 1995-2025, comprising 125,026 drug-AE associations. The time-indexed reference dataset, restricted to active products, included 1,479 medicinal products and 110,823 drug-AE associations. Most AEs were identified pre-marketing (74.5%) versus post-marketing (25.5%). Safety updates peaked around 2012. Gastrointestinal, skin, and nervous system disorders were the most represented System Organ Classes. Drugs had a median of 48 AEs across 14 SOCs. Conclusions: The proposed dataset addresses a critical gap in pharmacovigilance by incorporating temporal information on AE recognition for the EU, supporting more accurate assessment of signal detection performance and facilitating methodological comparisons across analytical approaches.

</details>


### [134] [When Perplexity Lies: Generation-Focused Distillation of Hybrid Sequence Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26556)
*Juan Gabriel Kostelec,Xiang Wang,Axel Laborieux,Christos Sourmpis,Qinghai Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Hybrid-KDA的高效Transformer蒸馏架构及GenDistill多阶段蒸馏流程，强调使用生成式评估而非仅依赖对数似然来衡量模型质量，发现在多个设计因素中数据集选择、完成掩码和注意力层冻结对生成质量影响最大，最终模型在降低75% KV缓存的同时保持教师模型86%-90%的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于对数似然评分的蒸馏模型评估方法无法准确反映自回归生成能力，导致模型设计决策可能被误导，因此需要一种更贴近实际生成任务的评估与设计框架。

Method: 提出Hybrid Kimi Delta Attention（Hybrid-KDA）架构与GenDistill多阶段蒸馏流程，并在整个过程中采用生成式评估；通过系统消融实验分析六个设计维度的影响：训练目标、损失掩码、训练时长、数据集选择、参数冻结和架构选择。

Result: 发现基于对数似然的评估会低估学生模型与教师模型之间的差距，甚至可能颠倒设计选择的优劣排序；其中数据集选择、仅完成掩码（completion-only masking）和后训练中冻结注意力层对生成质量影响最大。

Conclusion: 生成式评估对于构建高效的蒸馏模型至关重要，仅依赖困惑度或对数似然评估可能导致错误的设计结论；所提出的Hybrid-KDA模型在128K上下文下将首 token 时间提升2-4倍，KV缓存减少达75%，并在知识类基准上保留教师模型86%-90%的准确性。

Abstract: Converting a pretrained Transformer into a more efficient hybrid model through distillation offers a promising approach to reducing inference costs. However, achieving high-quality generation in distilled models requires careful joint design of both the student architecture and the distillation process. Many prior distillation works evaluate downstream multiple-choice benchmarks by ranking candidate answers with log-likelihood rather than requiring autoregressive generation, which can obscure important differences in model quality. For example, we show that a 7B parameter distilled model that nearly matches its teacher to within 0.2\,pp under log-likelihood scoring actually falls behind by 20.8\,pp when the model must generate answers autoregressively.
  We propose a Hybrid Kimi Delta Attention (Hybrid-KDA) architecture paired with GenDistill, a multi-stage distillation pipeline, and use generation-based evaluation throughout to guide design decisions. Applying this approach to Qwen3-0.6B, we systematically ablate six design axes: training objective, loss masking, training duration, dataset selection, parameter freezing, and architecture choice. We find that log-likelihood-based evaluation consistently underestimates the gap between teacher and student, and can in some cases reverse the ranking of design choices, meaning that conclusions drawn from perplexity-only evaluation may be misleading. Among the factors we study, dataset selection, completion-only masking, and freezing attention layers during post-training have the largest impact on generation quality.
  Our best Hybrid-KDA model retains 86--90\% of teacher accuracy on knowledge benchmarks while reducing KV cache memory by up to 75\% and improving time-to-first-token by 2--4$\times$ at 128K-token contexts.

</details>


### [135] [MemBoost: A Memory-Boosted Framework for Cost-Aware LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26557)
*Joris Köster,Zixuan Liu,Siavash Khajavi,Zizhan Zheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MemBoost是一种内存增强的LLM服务框架，通过复用历史回答和选择性调用强模型来降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在处理重复或近似查询时推理成本高，影响实际应用效率。

Method: 提出MemBoost框架，利用轻量模型复用先前生成的答案并检索相关信息，同时将复杂或不确定的查询转发给更强的模型处理；支持答案复用、持续内存增长和成本感知路由。

Result: 在多个模型和模拟工作负载下的实验表明，MemBoost显著减少了对昂贵大模型的调用和整体推理成本，同时保持了接近强模型基线的高质量回答。

Conclusion: MemBoost有效平衡了推理成本与回答质量，适用于存在大量重复查询的交互式LLM服务场景。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) deliver strong performance but incur high inference cost in real-world services, especially under workloads with repeated or near-duplicate queries across users and sessions. In this work, we propose MemBoost, a memory-boosted LLM serving framework that enables a lightweight model to reuse previously generated answers and retrieve relevant supporting information for cheap inference, while selectively escalating difficult or uncertain queries to a stronger model. Unlike standard retrieval-augmented generation, which primarily grounds a single response, MemBoost is designed for interactive settings by supporting answer reuse, continual memory growth, and cost-aware routing. Experiments across multiple models under simulated workloads show that MemBoost substantially reduces expensive large-model invocations and overall inference cost, while maintaining high answer quality comparable to the strong model baseline.

</details>


### [136] [EnTaCs: Analyzing the Relationship Between Sentiment and Language Choice in English-Tamil Code-Switching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26587)
*Paul Bontempo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究利用机器学习和统计模型分析英语-泰米尔语混用文本中情感与语言选择的关系，发现正面情绪的评论使用更多英语，而混合情感评论的语言转换频率更高，表明情感内容显著影响多语言环境中的语言选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨在多语言代码转换环境中，情感内容如何影响语言选择。

Method: 采用微调的XLM-RoBERTa模型对DravidianCodeMix数据集中的35,650条罗马化YouTube评论进行词级语言识别，并通过线性回归分析情感与语言使用比例及切换频率的关系。

Result: 正面情绪评论的英语使用比例（34.3%）显著高于负面情绪评论（24.8%），混合情感评论在控制语句长度后表现出最高的语言切换频率。

Conclusion: 情感内容显著影响多语种混用中的语言选择，这可能与嵌入语言和社会语言学中的声望及身份认同有关。

Abstract: This paper investigates the relationship between utterance sentiment and language choice in English-Tamil code-switched text, using methods from machine learning and statistical modelling. We apply a fine-tuned XLM-RoBERTa model for token-level language identification on 35,650 romanized YouTube comments from the DravidianCodeMix dataset, producing per-utterance measurements of English proportion and language switch frequency. Linear regression analysis reveals that positive utterances exhibit significantly greater English proportion (34.3%) than negative utterances (24.8%), and mixed-sentiment utterances show the highest language switch frequency when controlling for utterance length. These findings support the hypothesis that emotional content demonstrably influences language choice in multilingual code-switching settings, due to socio-linguistic associations of prestige and identity with embedded and matrix languages.

</details>


### [137] [Weight Tying Biases Token Embeddings Towards the Output Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26663)
*Antonio Lopardo,Avyukth Harish,Catherine Arnett,Akshat Gupta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了语言模型中权重绑定（即输入和输出嵌入矩阵共享参数）的影响，发现这种做法使嵌入矩阵更偏向于输出预测而非输入表示，从而损害了早期层的计算效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管权重绑定在语言模型中被广泛使用，但其对学习到的嵌入空间的影响尚不明确，本文旨在揭示其背后的机制。

Method: 通过比较绑定与非绑定模型中的嵌入矩阵，使用调整透镜（tuned lens）分析方法，并分析训练初期的梯度分布，探究权重绑定带来的偏差及其成因。

Result: 发现绑定的嵌入矩阵更接近输出解嵌矩阵而非输入嵌入矩阵，且输出梯度在训练初期占主导地位，导致输入表示能力下降；增强输入梯度可缓解该问题。

Conclusion: 权重绑定主要优化了输出预测性能，但牺牲了输入表示质量，这解释了为何在大规模模型中该方法可能损害整体性能，并对小型语言模型的设计具有指导意义。

Abstract: Weight tying, i.e. sharing parameters between input and output embedding matrices, is common practice in language model design, yet its impact on the learned embedding space remains poorly understood. In this paper, we show that tied embedding matrices align more closely with output (unembedding) matrices than with input embeddings of comparable untied models, indicating that the shared matrix is shaped primarily for output prediction rather than input representation. This unembedding bias arises because output gradients dominate early in training. Using tuned lens analysis, we show this negatively affects early-layer computations, which contribute less effectively to the residual stream. Scaling input gradients during training reduces this bias, providing causal evidence for the role of gradient imbalance. This is mechanistic evidence that weight tying optimizes the embedding matrix for output prediction, compromising its role in input representation. These results help explain why weight tying can harm performance at scale and have implications for training smaller LLMs, where the embedding matrix contributes substantially to total parameter count.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [138] [ETA-VLA: Efficient Token Adaptation via Temporal Fusion and Intra-LLM Sparsification for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25766)
*Yiru Wang,Anqing Jiang,Shuo Wang,Yuwen Heng,Zichong Gu,Hao Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的高效令牌适应框架ETA-VLA，通过引入ILSA模块动态剪枝冗余视觉令牌，显著降低计算开销，同时保持接近最先进的驾驶性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于大语言模型中自注意力机制的二次复杂性，传统VLA模型在处理多视角历史帧时面临严重的计算负担，限制了其在自动驾驶中的实时应用。

Method: 提出ETA-VLA框架，包含一个新的Intra-LLM Sparse Aggregator (ILSA) 模块，利用文本引导的评分机制和保持多样性的稀疏化策略，动态识别并剪枝冗余视觉令牌，仅保留对驾驶决策最关键的令牌。

Result: 在NAVSIM v2数据集上实验表明，ETA-VLA相比现有方法减少了约32%的计算FLOPs；在剪枝85%视觉令牌、推理FLOPs降低61%的情况下，仍保持原始精度的94%。

Conclusion: ETA-VLA通过高效的令牌选择机制，在保证驾驶性能的同时大幅降低了计算成本，为VLA模型在自动驾驶中的部署提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: The integration of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models into autonomous driving systems offers a unified framework for interpreting complex scenes and executing control commands. However, the necessity to incorporate historical multi-view frames for accurate temporal reasoning imposes a severe computational burden, primarily driven by the quadratic complexity of self-attention mechanisms in Large Language Models (LLMs). To alleviate this bottleneck, we propose ETA-VLA, an Efficient Token Adaptation framework for VLA models. ETA-VLA processes the past $n$ frames of multi-view images and introduces a novel Intra-LLM Sparse Aggregator (ILSA). Drawing inspiration from human driver attention allocation, ILSA dynamically identifies and prunes redundant visual tokens guided by textual queries and temporal consistency. Specifically, we utilize a text-guided scoring mechanism alongside a diversity-preserving sparsification strategy to select a sparse subset of critical tokens, ensuring comprehensive awareness of the driving scene. Extensive experiments on the NAVSIM v2 demonstrate that ETA-VLA achieves driving performance comparable to state-of-the-art baselines while reducing computational FLOPs by approximately 32\%. Notably, our method prunes 85% of visual tokens and reduces inference FLOPs by 61\%, but still retaining 94% of the original accuracy on the NAVSIM v2 benchmark.

</details>


### [139] [Massive Parallel Deep Reinforcement Learning for Active SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25834)
*Martín Arce Llobera,Julio A. Placed,Mariano De Paula,Pablo De Cristóforis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种可扩展的端到端深度强化学习框架，用于主动SLAM，支持大规模并行训练，显著减少训练时间，支持连续动作空间，并促进更真实场景的探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于深度强化学习（DRL）的主动SLAM方法受限于缺乏可扩展的并行训练，难以应对计算密集型任务。

Method: 设计并实现了一个支持大规模并行训练的端到端DRL框架，利用并行计算和GPU加速技术优化训练效率。

Result: 相比现有方法，该框架显著减少了训练时间，支持连续动作空间，并能够在更真实的场景中进行探索。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架不仅提升了主动SLAM的训练效率和应用范围，还作为开源项目发布，促进了研究的可重复性和社区发展。

Abstract: Recent advances in parallel computing and GPU acceleration have created new opportunities for computation-intensive learning problems such as Active SLAM -- where actions are selected to reduce uncertainty and improve joint mapping and localization. However, existing DRL-based approaches remain constrained by the lack of scalable parallel training. In this work, we address this challenge by proposing a scalable end-to-end DRL framework for Active SLAM that enables massively parallel training. Compared with the state of the art, our method significantly reduces training time, supports continuous action spaces and facilitates the exploration of more realistic scenarios. It is released as an open-source framework to promote reproducibility and community adoption.

</details>


### [140] [Chasing Autonomy: Dynamic Retargeting and Control Guided RL for Performant and Controllable Humanoid Running](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25902)
*Zachary Olkin,William D. Compton,Ryan M. Bena,Aaron D. Ames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过优化流程动态重定向人类运动的方法，生成改进的周期性参考库，并结合目标条件和控制引导的奖励机制，实现了人形机器人在真实环境中的高速、长距离自主奔跑与避障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习控制器虽然能模仿人类动态运动，但通常局限于单一动作回放，难以实现长时间、自主的运动控制，因此需要一种能够从单个人类演示中生成多样化且优化的运动参考的方法。

Method: 提出一个包含硬约束优化过程的流水线，用于动态重定向人类运动并生成改进的周期性参考库；研究参考运动和奖励结构对速度跟踪性能的影响，采用目标条件化和控制引导的奖励机制来跟踪经过动态优化的人类运动数据。

Result: 在Unitree G1机器人上实现了最高3.3 m/s的奔跑速度，能够在真实环境中行走数百米，并集成到完整的感知与规划自主系统中，实现户外奔跑时的动态避障。

Conclusion: 结合动态优化的参考运动和目标-控制联合引导的奖励设计，显著提升了人形机器人在复杂环境下的动态运动能力与自主性，为实现类人高速持久 locomotion 提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Humanoid robots have the promise of locomoting like humans, including fast and dynamic running. Recently, reinforcement learning (RL) controllers that can mimic human motions have become popular as they can generate very dynamic behaviors, but they are often restricted to single motion play-back which hinders their deployment in long duration and autonomous locomotion. In this paper, we present a pipeline to dynamically retarget human motions through an optimization routine with hard constraints to generate improved periodic reference libraries from a single human demonstration. We then study the effect of both the reference motion and the reward structure on the reference and commanded velocity tracking, concluding that a goal-conditioned and control-guided reward which tracks dynamically optimized human data results in the best performance. We deploy the policy on hardware, demonstrating its speed and endurance by achieving running speeds of up to 3.3 m/s on a Unitree G1 robot and traversing hundreds of meters in real-world environments. Additionally, to demonstrate the controllability of the locomotion, we use the controller in a full perception and planning autonomy stack for obstacle avoidance while running outdoors.

</details>


### [141] [Can Vision Foundation Models Navigate? Zero-Shot Real-World Evaluation and Lessons Learned](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25937)
*Maeva Guerrier,Karthik Soma,Jana Pavlasek,Giovanni Beltrame*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对五种先进的视觉导航模型（VNMs）在真实世界中的表现进行了全面评估，超越了传统的成功率指标，引入了基于路径和视觉目标识别的度量，并通过图像扰动测试鲁棒性。研究揭示了这些模型在几何理解、位置区分能力和分布外泛化方面的系统性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对视觉导航模型的评估主要依赖于成功率，无法反映轨迹质量、碰撞行为及环境变化下的鲁棒性，因此需要更全面的评估方法。

Method: 在两个机器人平台和五个室内外环境中评估五种最先进的VNMs；结合路径-based指标与视觉目标识别得分，并通过受控图像扰动（如运动模糊、阳光 flare）测试模型鲁棒性。

Result: 发现三大系统性问题：1）即使复杂的扩散和Transformer模型也频繁发生碰撞，显示几何理解有限；2）模型难以区分外观相似但语义不同的位置，导致重复环境中的目标预测错误；3）在分布偏移下性能下降。

Conclusion: 当前VNMs在真实场景中仍存在显著局限，需改进几何推理、语义感知与鲁棒性；作者将公开代码库和数据集以促进可复现的基准测试。

Abstract: Visual Navigation Models (VNMs) promise generalizable, robot navigation by learning from large-scale visual demonstrations. Despite growing real-world deployment, existing evaluations rely almost exclusively on success rate, whether the robot reaches its goal, which conceals trajectory quality, collision behavior, and robustness to environmental change. We present a real-world evaluation of five state-of-the-art VNMs (GNM, ViNT, NoMaD, NaviBridger, and CrossFormer) across two robot platforms and five environments spanning indoor and outdoor settings. Beyond success rate, we combine path-based metrics with vision-based goal-recognition scores and assess robustness through controlled image perturbations (motion blur, sunflare). Our analysis uncovers three systematic limitations: (a) even architecturally sophisticated diffusion and transformer-based models exhibit frequent collisions, indicating limited geometric understanding; (b) models fail to discriminate between different locations that are perceptually similar, however some semantics differences are present, causing goal prediction errors in repetitive environments; and (c) performance degrades under distribution shift. We will publicly release our evaluation codebase and dataset to facilitate reproducible benchmarking of VNMs.

</details>


### [142] [Line-of-Sight-Constrained Multi-Robot Mapless Navigation via Polygonal Visible Regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26314)
*Ruofei Bai,Shenghai Yuan,Xinhang Xu,Xingyu Ji,Xiaowei Li,Hongliang Guo,Wei-Yun Yau,Lihua Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分布式多机器人导航中的视线连通性维护框架，利用实时LiDAR扫描构建个体可视区域，通过共享信息实现连通性保障，并引入优化的LoS距离度量以提升鲁棒性和导航效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常依赖已知环境地图来建模视线约束，限制了在未知障碍环境中的实际部署，因此需要一种不依赖全局地图的分布式解决方案。

Method: 每个机器人基于实时LiDAR数据构建自我中心的可视区域，通过分布式通信共享这些区域以建立机器人间的视线约束，并将其集成到导航框架中；同时提出更精确的LoS距离度量以支持灵活的拓扑优化。

Result: 在仿真和真实实验中验证了该框架能在复杂障碍环境中可靠维持视线连通性，即使在大视野范围和脆弱的最小拓扑下也优于现有方法，消融实验显示拓扑优化使导航效率提升约20%。

Conclusion: 所提方法实现了在未知环境中高效、鲁棒的多机器人视线连通性维护，具备良好的实用潜力。

Abstract: Multi-robot systems rely on underlying connectivity to ensure reliable communication and timely coordination. This paper studies the line-of-sight (LoS) connectivity maintenance problem in multi-robot navigation with unknown obstacles. Prior works typically assume known environment maps to formulate LoS constraints between robots, which hinders their practical deployment. To overcome this limitation, we propose an inherently distributed approach where each robot only constructs an egocentric visible region based on its real-time LiDAR scans, instead of endeavoring to build a global map online. The individual visible regions are shared through distributed communication to establish inter-robot LoS constraints, which are then incorporated into a multi-robot navigation framework to ensure LoS-connectivity. Moreover, we enhance the robustness of connectivity maintenance by proposing a more accurate LoS-distance metric, which further enables flexible topology optimization that eliminates redundant and effort-demanding connections. The proposed framework is evaluated through extensive multi-robot navigation and exploration tasks in both simulation and real-world experiments. Results show that it reliably maintains LoS-connectivity between robots in challenging environments cluttered with obstacles, even under large visible ranges and fragile minimal topologies, where existing methods consistently fail. Ablation studies also reveal that topology optimization boosts navigation efficiency by around $20\%$, demonstrating the framework's potential for efficient navigation under connectivity constraints.

</details>


### [143] [DFM-VLA: Iterative Action Refinement for Robot Manipulation via Discrete Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26320)
*Jiayi Chen,Wenxuan Song,Shuai Chen,Jingbo Wang,Zhijun Li,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DFM-VLA，一种基于离散流匹配的视觉-语言-动作模型，通过迭代优化动作标记序列，克服现有方法中动作标记生成后无法修正的问题，在多个操作任务上实现了优于自回归和扩散模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作模型在动作解码时一旦生成标记便无法修改，导致早期错误难以纠正，限制了操纵性能。因此需要一种能够迭代优化动作序列的方法。

Method: 提出DFM-VLA，建模标记级概率速度场以动态更新整个动作序列；探索了两种构建速度场的方式：辅助速度头和动作嵌入引导；采用两阶段解码策略，包括迭代优化和确定性验证，以实现稳定收敛。

Result: 在CALVIN、LIBERO和真实世界操作任务上的实验表明，DFM-VLA在操作性能上持续优于强基线模型，且保持高推理效率；在CALVIN上平均成功长度为4.44，在LIBERO上平均成功率高达95.7%。

Conclusion: 离散流匹配结合迭代优化能有效提升VLA模型的动作预测准确性，DFM-VLA为机器人操纵提供了一种高效且高性能的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision--Language--Action (VLA) models that encode actions using a discrete tokenization scheme are increasingly adopted for robotic manipulation, but existing decoding paradigms remain fundamentally limited. Whether actions are decoded sequentially by autoregressive VLAs or in parallel by discrete diffusion VLAs, once a token is generated, it is typically fixed and cannot be revised in subsequent iterations, so early token errors cannot be effectively corrected later. We propose DFM-VLA, a discrete flow matching VLA for iterative refinement of action tokens. DFM-VLA~models a token-level probability velocity field that dynamically updates the full action sequence across refinement iterations. We investigate two ways to construct the velocity field: an auxiliary velocity-head formulation and an action-embedding-guided formulation. Our framework further adopts a two-stage decoding strategy with an iterative refinement stage followed by deterministic validation for stable convergence. Extensive experiments on CALVIN, LIBERO, and real-world manipulation tasks show that DFM-VLA consistently outperforms strong autoregressive, discrete diffusion, and continuous diffusion baselines in manipulation performance while retaining high inference efficiency. In particular, DFM-VLA achieves an average success length of 4.44 on CALVIN and an average success rate of 95.7\% on LIBERO, highlighting the value of action refinement via discrete flow matching for robotic manipulation. Our project is available \url{https://chris1220313648.github.io/DFM-VLA/}

</details>


### [144] [DiffusionAnything: End-to-End In-context Diffusion Learning for Unified Navigation and Pre-Grasp Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26322)
*Iana Zhura,Yara Mahmoud,Jeffrin Sam,Hung Khang Nguyen,Didar Seyidov,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于图像空间扩散策略的统一模型，仅需5分钟自监督数据即可实现跨尺度的视觉驱动机器人运动规划，在无需语言模型和深度传感器的情况下实现了对新场景的零样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作模型依赖大量计算资源和标注数据，且难以在新场景中实现零样本迁移，同时多任务规划通常需要显式目标定义和任务特定设计，限制了实际应用中的效率与通用性。

Method: 引入一种多尺度特征调制的图像空间扩散策略，通过FiLM条件机制融合任务模式、深度尺度和空间注意力；结合轨迹对齐的深度预测实现度量级3D推理，并利用AnyTraverse提供的自监督注意力实现目标导向推断，仅使用RGB输入完成导航与操作任务。

Result: 该模型在每任务仅5分钟自监督数据下，以2.0 GB内存占用和10 Hz运行速度，实现了米级导航与厘米级操作的统一处理，展现出强健的零样本场景泛化能力。

Conclusion: 所提方法实现了高效、轻量且通用的视觉驱动机器人运动规划，无需语言模型或深度传感器，适合机载部署，推动了零样本、跨尺度具身智能的发展。

Abstract: Efficiently predicting motion plans directly from vision remains a fundamental challenge in robotics, where planning typically requires explicit goal specification and task-specific design. Recent vision-language-action (VLA) models infer actions directly from visual input but demand massive computational resources, extensive training data, and fail zero-shot in novel scenes. We present a unified image-space diffusion policy handling both meter-scale navigation and centimeter-scale manipulation via multi-scale feature modulation, with only 5 minutes of self-supervised data per task. Three key innovations drive the framework: (1) Multi-scale FiLM conditioning on task mode, depth scale, and spatial attention enables task-appropriate behavior in a single model; (2) trajectory-aligned depth prediction focuses metric 3D reasoning along generated waypoints; (3) self-supervised attention from AnyTraverse enables goal-directed inference without vision-language models and depth sensors. Operating purely from RGB input (2.0 GB memory, 10 Hz), the model achieves robust zero-shot generalization to novel scenes while remaining suitable for onboard deployment.

</details>


### [145] [Optimal Prioritized Dissipation and Closed-Form Damping Limitation under Actuator Constraints for Haptic Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26347)
*Camilla Celli,Andrea Bini,Valerio Novelli,Alessandro Filippeschi,Francesco Porcini,Antonio Frisoli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于优先耗散动作的阻尼限制方法，以在保证稳定性的前提下最小化执行器负载并最大化透明度，并通过实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稳定性控制方法（如时域无源法）过于保守，导致透明度下降，且未充分考虑各执行器的功率限制与物理约束之间的冲突。

Method: 设计一种优先耗散策略，优先选择最优耗散方向以最小化执行器负载，多余耗散分配到正交超平面，提供闭式解并适应多自由度及各向异性情况。

Result: 该方法在并联触觉界面与虚拟环境交互中得到实验验证，能在多种工况下有效保障稳定性并提升透明度。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效协调稳定性、透明度与执行器物理限制之间的矛盾，优于现有技术，适用于复杂多自由度触觉系统。

Abstract: In haptics, guaranteeing stability is essential to ensure safe interaction with remote or virtual environments. One of the most relevant methods at the state-of-the-art is the Time Domain Passivity Approach (TDPA). However, its high conservatism leads to a significant degradation of transparency. Moreover, the stabilizing action may conflict with the device's physical limitations. State-of-the-art solutions have attempted to address these actuator limits, but they still fail to account simultaneously for the power limits of each actuator while maximizing transparency. This work proposes a new damping limitation method based on prioritized dissipation actions. It prioritizes an optimal dissipation direction that minimizes actuator load, while any excess dissipation is allocated to the orthogonal hyperplane. The solution provides a closed-form formulation and is robust in multi-DoF scenarios, even in the presence of actuator and motion anisotropies. The method is experimentally validated using a parallel haptic interface interacting with a virtual environment and tested under different operating conditions.

</details>


### [146] [T-800: An 800 Hz Data Glove for Precise Hand Gesture Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26403)
*Haoyang Luo,Zihang Zhao,Leiyao Cui,Saiyao Zhang,Liu Yang,Zhi Han,Xiyuan Tang,Yixin Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: T-800是一种高带宽数据手套系统，可在800 Hz下实现全手同步运动追踪，揭示了人类灵巧操作中此前因采样率不足而无法捕捉的高频运动特征，并展示了其在机器人灵巧操作中的应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作捕捉技术在时间分辨率和视觉遮挡之间存在权衡，难以记录快速、接触丰富的精细手部运动，限制了对人类灵巧性高频动态的理解和在机器人中的应用。

Method: 提出T-800系统，采用广播式同步机制和机械应力隔离结构，实现18个分布式惯性测量单元在800 Hz下的亚帧级时间对齐同步追踪，并结合运动学重定向算法将高保真手势映射到机器人手上。

Result: T-800成功恢复了以往因时间欠采样而丢失的精细操作细节，发现人类灵巧操作包含显著的高频运动能量（>100 Hz），突破了传统硬件奈奎斯特采样极限的限制，并实现了向机器人手的精确运动映射。

Conclusion: T-800为研究人类高速灵巧操作提供了新工具，所获取的高频率行为数据有望用于训练更鲁棒的机器人控制策略，推动生物启发式机器人发展。

Abstract: Human dexterity relies on rapid, sub-second motor adjustments, yet capturing these high-frequency dynamics remains an enduring challenge in biomechanics and robotics. Existing motion capture paradigms are compromised by a trade-off between temporal resolution and visual occlusion, failing to record the fine-grained hand motion of fast, contact-rich manipulation. Here we introduce T-800, a high-bandwidth data glove system that achieves synchronized, full-hand motion tracking at 800 Hz. By integrating a novel broadcast-based synchronization mechanism with a mechanical stress isolation architecture, our system maintains sub-frame temporal alignment across 18 distributed inertial measurement units (IMUs) during extended, vigorous movements. We demonstrate that T-800 recovers fine-grained manipulation details previously lost to temporal undersampling. Our analysis reveals that human dexterity exhibits significantly high-frequency motion energy (>100 Hz) that was fundamentally inaccessible due to the Nyquist sampling limit imposed by previous hardware constraints. To validate the system's utility for robotic manipulation, we implement a kinematic retargeting algorithm that maps T-800's high-fidelity human gestures onto dexterous robotic hand models. This demonstrates that the high-frequency motion data can be accurately translated while respecting the kinematic constraints of robotic hands, providing the rich behavioral data necessary for training robust control policies in the future.

</details>


### [147] [Generalizable task-oriented object grasping through LLM-guided ontology and similarity-based planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26412)
*Hao Chen,Takuya Kiyokawa,Weiwei Wan,Kensuke Harada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种几何中心的任务导向抓取方法，通过大语言模型构建物体部件-任务本体，基于点云的几何分析识别功能部件，并利用相似物体进行模仿抓取规划，提升了对新物体和任务的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在部件识别和抓取推断上不稳定，依赖语义特征且对视角敏感，难以泛化到多样化的物体和任务。

Method: 1) 利用大语言模型构建物体-部件-任务本体；2) 基于多点分布和距离度量的采样式几何分析方法，从点云中识别目标部件；3) 使用已知相似物体的分割与抓取知识，通过相似性匹配实现模仿抓取规划。

Result: 实验验证了该方法在功能部件选择、识别和抓取生成上的高精度，并展示了其对新类别物体的良好泛化能力。

Conclusion: 所提几何中心策略不依赖视觉语义识别，有效克服了视角敏感性，显著提升了任务导向抓取在多样化对象和任务中的通用性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Task-oriented grasping (TOG) is more challenging than simple object grasping because it requires precise identification of object parts and careful selection of grasping areas to ensure effective and robust manipulation. While recent approaches have trained large-scale vision-language models to integrate part-level object segmentation with task-aware grasp planning, their instability in part recognition and grasp inference limits their ability to generalize across diverse objects and tasks. To address this issue, we introduce a novel, geometry-centric strategy for more generalizable TOG that does not rely on semantic features from visual recognition, effectively overcoming the viewpoint sensitivity of model-based approaches. Our main proposals include: 1) an object-part-task ontology for functional part selection based on intuitive human commands, constructed using a Large Language Model (LLM); 2) a sampling-based geometric analysis method for identifying the selected object part from observed point clouds, incorporating multiple point distribution and distance metrics; and 3) a similarity matching framework for imitative grasp planning, utilizing similar known objects with pre-existing segmentation and grasping knowledge as references to guide the planning for unknown targets. We validate the high accuracy of our approach in functional part selection, identification, and grasp generation through real-world experiments. Additionally, we demonstrate the method's generalization capabilities to novel-category objects by extending existing ontological knowledge, showcasing its adaptability to a broad range of objects and tasks.

</details>


### [148] [120 Minutes and a Laptop: Minimalist Image-goal Navigation via Unsupervised Exploration and Offline RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26441)
*Xiaoming Liu,Borong Zhang,Qingbiao Li,Steven Morad*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MINav是一种高效的图像目标导航方法，可在120分钟内在消费级笔记本上完成数据收集、训练和部署，无需人工干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战现有依赖大规模数据集和高计算资源的图像目标导航范式，探索低门槛、高效率的机器人学习方法。

Method: 将图像目标导航建模为离线目标条件强化学习问题，结合无监督数据收集、回溯目标重标记和离线策略学习。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界实验中，MINav提升了探索效率，优于零样本导航基线，并随数据集规模良好扩展。

Conclusion: 高效的真实世界机器人学习可在低计算资源下实现，降低快速策略原型设计与部署的门槛。

Abstract: The prevailing paradigm for image-goal visual navigation often assumes access to large-scale datasets, substantial pretraining, and significant computational resources. In this work, we challenge this assumption. We show that we can collect a dataset, train an in-domain policy, and deploy it to the real world (1) in less than 120 minutes, (2) on a consumer laptop, (3) without any human intervention. Our method, MINav, formulates image-goal navigation as an offline goal-conditioned reinforcement learning problem, combining unsupervised data collection with hindsight goal relabeling and offline policy learning. Experiments in simulation and the real world show that MINav improves exploration efficiency, outperforms zero-shot navigation baselines in target environments, and scales favorably with dataset size. These results suggest that effective real-world robotic learning can be achieved with high computational efficiency, lowering the barrier to rapid policy prototyping and deployment.

</details>


### [149] [DTP-Attack: A decision-based black-box adversarial attack on trajectory prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26462)
*Jiaxiang Li,Jun Yan,Daniel Watzenig,Huilin Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DTP-Attack，一种面向轨迹预测系统的决策型黑盒对抗攻击框架，仅依赖二值决策输出即可实现高成功率的意图误分类与预测精度降级攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗攻击方法多需白盒访问权限且受限于刚性物理约束，难以在真实场景中对轨迹预测系统实施有效攻击，限制了其实际应用价值。

Method: 提出DTP-Attack，采用新型边界行走算法，在无固定约束条件下探索对抗区域，通过保持轨迹邻近性来维持生成轨迹的真实性，并仅利用模型的二值决策输出进行攻击，适用于黑盒环境。

Result: 在nuScenes和Apolloscape数据集上对Trajectron++、Grip++等先进模型的实验表明，DTP-Attack在意图误分类攻击中达到41%-81%的成功率（扰动小于0.45米），并在精度降级攻击中使预测误差增加1.9-4.2，显著优于现有黑盒方法。

Conclusion: 当前轨迹预测系统存在根本性安全漏洞，DTP-Attack展示了在现实可行条件下实施高效黑盒攻击的可能性，凸显了发展强健防御机制的紧迫性。

Abstract: Trajectory prediction systems are critical for autonomous vehicle safety, yet remain vulnerable to adversarial attacks that can cause catastrophic traffic behavior misinterpretations. Existing attack methods require white-box access with gradient information and rely on rigid physical constraints, limiting real-world applicability. We propose DTP-Attack, a decision-based black-box adversarial attack framework tailored for trajectory prediction systems. Our method operates exclusively on binary decision outputs without requiring model internals or gradients, making it practical for real-world scenarios. DTP-Attack employs a novel boundary walking algorithm that navigates adversarial regions without fixed constraints, naturally maintaining trajectory realism through proximity preservation. Unlike existing approaches, our method supports both intention misclassification attacks and prediction accuracy degradation. Extensive evaluation on nuScenes and Apolloscape datasets across state-of-the-art models including Trajectron++ and Grip++ demonstrates superior performance. DTP-Attack achieves 41 - 81% attack success rates for intention misclassification attacks that manipulate perceived driving maneuvers with perturbations below 0.45 m, and increases prediction errors by 1.9 - 4.2 for accuracy degradation. Our method consistently outperforms existing black-box approaches while maintaining high controllability and reliability across diverse scenarios. These results reveal fundamental vulnerabilities in current trajectory prediction systems, highlighting urgent needs for robust defenses in safety-critical autonomous driving applications.

</details>


### [150] [Adapt as You Say: Online Interactive Bimanual Skill Adaptation via Human Language Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26466)
*Zhuo Li,Dianxi Li,Tao Teng,Quentin Rouxel,Zhipeng Dong,Dennis Hong,Darwin Caldwell,Fei Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为BiSAIL的新型框架，通过交互式语言反馈实现双手机械技能的零样本在线自适应，显著提升了在人类参与下的适应性、任务泛化能力和跨平台扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 使通用机器人能够在不断变化的任务条件下自主适应，特别是在人类生活环境中灵活执行双手机械操作仍是一个挑战。

Method: 采用分层的“推理-调制”范式，首先从多模态任务变化中推断出通用的适应目标，然后通过扩散调制调整双手机械运动以实现这些目标。

Result: 在六项双手机械任务和两个双臂平台上进行了大量真实机器人实验，结果表明BiSAIL在人类参与的适应性、任务泛化和跨具身可扩展性方面显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: BiSAIL使得非专家用户可以通过直观的语言指令灵活定制自适应的双手机器人助手，推动了通用机器人在动态环境中的实际应用。

Abstract: Developing general-purpose robots capable of autonomously operating in human living environments requires the ability to adapt to continuously evolving task conditions. However, adapting high-dimensional coordinated bimanual skills to novel task variations at deployment remains a fundamental challenge. In this work, we present BiSAIL (Bimanual Skill Adaptation via Interactive Language), a novel framework that enables zero-shot online adaptation of offline-learned bimanual skills through interactive language feedback. The key idea of BiSAIL is to adopt a hierarchical reason-then-modulate paradigm, which first infers generalized adaptation objectives from multimodal task variations, and then adapts bimanual motions via diffusion modulation to achieve the inferred objectives. Extensive real-robot experiments across six bimanual tasks and two dual-arm platforms demonstrate that BiSAIL significantly outperforms existing methods in human-in-the-loop adaptability, task generalization and cross-embodiment scalability. This work enables the development of adaptive bimanual assistants that can be flexibly customized by non-expert users via intuitive verbal corrections. Experimental videos and code are available at https://rip4kobe.github.io/BiSAIL/.

</details>


### [151] [Addressing Ambiguity in Imitation Learning through Product of Experts based Negative Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26467)
*John Bateman,Andy M. Tyrrell,Jihong Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用次优示范和负反馈来提升机器人在模糊任务中学习效果的新方法，相较于传统模仿学习显著提高了成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多现实应用中，专家示范往往难以获得，且用户提供的示范质量参差不齐，因此需要一种能有效利用非理想示范并从失败中学习的系统。

Method: 提出一种基于负反馈的模仿学习框架，通过结合自身失败经验与次优示范进行学习，并在模拟和真实机器人环境中验证其有效性。

Result: 该方法在模拟环境中比无负反馈系统成功率提高90%，在真实机器人上提高50%，且在效率、内存和时间开销方面优于对比方案。

Conclusion: 引入负反馈机制可显著增强机器人从非理想示范中学习的能力，尤其适用于家庭助手机器人等存在任务模糊性的实际场景。

Abstract: Programming robots to perform complex tasks is often difficult and time consuming, requiring expert knowledge and skills in robot software and sometimes hardware. Imitation learning is a method for training robots to perform tasks by leveraging human expertise through demonstrations. Typically, the assumption is that those demonstrations are performed by a single, highly competent expert. However, in many real-world applications that use user demonstrations for tasks or incorporate both user data and pretrained data, such as home robotics including assistive robots, this is unlikely to be the case. This paper presents research towards a system which can leverage suboptimal demonstrations to solve ambiguous tasks; and particularly learn from its own failures. This is a negative-feedback system which achieves significant improvement over purely positive imitation learning for ambiguous tasks, achieving a 90% improvement in success rate against a system that does not utilise negative feedback, compared to a 50% improvement in success rate when utilised on a real robot, as well as demonstrating higher efficacy, memory efficiency and time efficiency than a comparable negative feedback scheme. The novel scheme presented in this paper is validated through simulated and real-robot experiments.

</details>


### [152] [The Multi-AMR Buffer Storage, Retrieval, and Reshuffling Problem: Exact and Heuristic Approaches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26542)
*Max Disselnmeyer,Thomas Bömer,Laura Dörr,Bastian Amberg,Anne Meyer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了多自主移动机器人（Multi-AMR）缓冲存储、检索与重排问题（BSRRP），以应对高密度生产环境中并发重排及带时间窗的存取任务。提出分层启发式算法，结合A*搜索与约束规划，显著降低计算时间，验证了其在实际应用中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多集中于固定物品集的重排与检索，难以应对实际制造中不断到达的货物单元。同时，劳动力短缺和运营成本上升促使密集存储环境需实现自动化，亟需一种能适应动态输入的缓冲区管理方案。

Method: 建立了二元整数规划（IP）模型用于基准测试，并提出一种分层启发式算法：上层采用A*算法进行任务级序列规划，下层使用约束规划（CP）实现多机器人协同调度。

Result: 实验表明，相比精确求解方法，所提启发式算法在计算时间上实现了数量级的减少，且可有效支持高密度生产环境下的实时响应控制。

Conclusion: 该分层方法为多AMR在复杂缓冲区操作中的应用提供了高效、可扩展的解决方案，具备在现实制造场景中部署的潜力。

Abstract: Buffer zones are essential in production systems to decouple sequential processes. In dense floor storage environments, such as space-constrained brownfield facilities, manual operation is increasingly challenged by severe labor shortages and rising operational costs. Automating these zones requires solving the Buffer Storage, Retrieval, and Reshuffling Problem (BSRRP). While previous work has addressed scenarios where the focus is limited to reshuffling and retrieving a fixed set of items, real-world manufacturing necessitates an adaptive approach that also incorporates arriving unit loads. This paper introduces the Multi-AMR BSRRP, coordinating a robot fleet to manage concurrent reshuffling, alongside time-windowed storage and retrieval tasks, within a shared floor area. We formulate a Binary Integer Programming (IP) model to obtain exact solutions for benchmarking purposes. As the problem is NP-hard, rendering exact methods computationally intractable for industrial scales, we propose a hierarchical heuristic. This approach decomposes the problem into an A* search for task-level sequence planning of unit load placements, and a Constraint Programming (CP) approach for multi-robot coordination and scheduling. Experiments demonstrate orders-of-magnitude computation time reductions compared to the exact formulation. These results confirm the heuristic's viability as responsive control logic for high-density production environments.

</details>
